东方金诚宏观研究

1 
10月宏观数据有所下行，新旧动能转换持续推进 
——2026年 10月宏观数据点评 
首席宏观分析师 王青  研究发展部执行总监 冯琳 
事件：据国家统计局2026年11月16日公布的数据，10月规模以上工业增加值同比实际增速为4.8%，
9月为5.5%，1-10月为5.3%，去年全年为5.9%；10月社会消费品零售总额同比增速为0.4%，9月为1.1%，
1-10月为 1.0%，去年全年为 3.7%；1-10月全国固定资产投资累计同比下降 8.1%，前值为-7.2%，去年全
年为-3.8%。 
基本观点： 
总体上看，主要受中秋错期导致居民消费增速回落，前期出台的稳投资政策需要传导时间，以及外
部扰动加剧等影响，10月各类宏观数据普遍有所下行。不过，当月高技术制造业生产增速加快，相关投
资保持正增长，居民消费中的旅游出行、文体休闲消费延续较快增长势头，显示新动能对宏观经济增长
的拉动力进一步增强。工业生产方面，10月工业增加值增速较上月放缓，“中秋错期”是直接扰动，同
时，内需偏弱与外部扰动加剧导致能源成本上行，进一步加大工业生产下行压力，但当月出口高增与新
动能行业生产较快扩张，仍对工业生产形成有力支撑。10月社零同比增速回落，主要受“中秋错期”因
素影响。同时，在补贴政策拉动效果减弱、房地产市场持续下行、居民消费信心不足等因素叠加影响下，
当月消费增长动能边际转弱，也是社零增速放缓的重要原因。1-10月固定资产投资同比增速为-8.1%，降
幅较前值扩大 0.9个百分点。9月以来稳投资政策全面发力，但从资金投放到形成实物工作量需要传导时
间，这是 10月基建投资、制造业投资未能遏制降幅扩大势头的主要原因。受近期新房市场延续深度调整
等影响，10月房地产投资下行加快，符合预期。不过，前10个月知识产权产品投资同比增长9.2%，高技
术产业投资保持较快正增长，显示投资结构优化。 
往后看，年底前出口仍然有望保持 20%以上的高增速，9月末以来系列稳增长政策出台后，接下来投
资降幅有收敛趋势，低基数下居民消费增速也将有所回升。这将带动工业生产增速上行，服务业生产将
保持较快增长，当前比较稳定的就业形势将会延续。初步估计，四季度 GDP增速将加快至 4.6%左右（三
季度为 4.5%），全年 GDP增速将在 4.6%附近，落在“4.5%至5.0%”的目标范围之内。由此我们预计，尽
管 10月宏观数据有所回落，但年底前出台新一轮稳增长政策的迫切性不高；着眼于稳定明年一季度宏观
经济运行，后期包括全面降息在内，各类总量型稳增长政策都有发力空间。

### Page 2

东方金诚宏观研究

2 
   具体解读如下： 
一、10月工业生产增速较上月明显放缓，“中秋错期”是直接扰动，同时，内需偏弱与能源成本上
行进一步加大生产下行压力；但出口高增与新动能行业生产较快扩张，仍对工业生产形成有力支撑。  
10月规上工业增加值同比增长 4.8%，增速较上月回落 0.7个百分点。分门类看，采矿业、制造业、
电力燃气及水的生产和供应业增加值同比分别增长0.7%、5.4%和4.4%，增速较上月分别放缓0.6、0.6和
1.2个百分点。 
10月工业生产增速放缓，主要受三方面因素影响：一是“中秋错期”导致今年 9月工作日比去年同
期多一天，而 10月工作日天数则与去年 10月相同，工业生产同比增速相应回落。二是 10月国内投资降
幅扩大、消费增速放缓，内需对工业生产构成一定抑制。三是 10月外部扰动加剧，国际原油价格上涨，
国内煤炭价格也有一定幅度上行，成本上升对部分行业生产形成压力。这主要体现在当月化学原料及化
学制品制造业增加值同比增速从上月的 2.4%放缓至 1.3%，以及从今年 4月起，石油、煤炭及其他燃料加
工业增加值已连续6个月同比负增。 
不过，与投资和消费走势相比，10 月工业生产、尤其是制造业生产仍维持较快增长。这主要受出口
高增以及新动能领域生产持续较快增长拉动。一方面，10月规上企业出口交货值同比增长18.6%，增速较
上月加快 1.6个百分点，对生产的拉动进一步增强。当月出口高增主要受集成电路出口带动，在此背景
下，10月计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增加值同比增速较上月加快3.0个百分点至19.4%；另一方
面，在市场供需两旺、财政金融政策支持力度持续加大的有利环境下，10 月高技术产业增加值同比增长
17.6%，增速较上月加快 2.3个百分点，装备制造业增加值同比增长 10.6%，均显著高于规上工业增加值
总体增速，对整体工业生产形成较为强劲的拉动作用。 
展望 11月，出口将延续高增，国内稳增长政策发力有望带动投资改善，高技术制造业生产将延续较
快增长，叠加今年 11月工作日比去年同期多一天，工业生产增速总体上将有所回升，预计当月工业增加
值同比增速将小幅加快至5.0%左右。 
三、10月社零同比增速回落，主要受“中秋错期”因素影响。同时，在补贴政策拉动效果减弱、房
地产市场持续下行、居民消费信心不足等因素叠加影响下，当月消费增长动能边际转弱，也是社零增速
放缓的重要原因。 
10月社零同比增长 0.4%，增速较上月回落 0.7个百分点。分项看，商品零售和餐饮收入同比分别增
长0.3%和0.7%，增速较上月分别放缓0.7和1.4个百分点。当月社零增速明显回落，主要受“中秋错期”

### Page 3

东方金诚宏观研究

3 
影响。去年中秋假期落在 10月上旬并与国庆连休，今年则位于 9月中旬，假日消费在两个月份之间重新
分布，对 9月同比形成正向支撑，对 10月同比则产生一定下拉作用。同时，从环比来看，10月社零季调
环比增长 0.02%，增速低于 9月的 0.35%，也低于过去十年同期 0.45%的平均水平，显示消费增长动能依
然偏弱，这主要受补贴政策拉动效果减弱、房地产市场持续下行、居民消费信心不足等因素叠加影响。 
从限额以上商品零售额看，主要受中秋错期影响，10 月限额以上各类别商品零售额增速普遍放缓。
必需消费品中，粮油食品、中西药品零售额同比增速分别降至0.5%（前值6.7%）和3.7%（前值5.0%），
日用品零售额同比下降 0.4%（前值增长 4.1%）。可选消费品中，化妆品、通讯器材零售额同比增速分别
回落至2.8%（前值5.4%）和6.5%（前值31.1%）；服装鞋帽零售额同比下降2.0%（前值增长1.2%）；金
银珠宝、汽车零售额同比降幅分别扩大至 27.7%和 21.4%。不过，受去年同期基数走低影响，10月家电零
售额同比增速加快至 2.5%（前值 1.9%），体育娱乐用品零售额同比增长 1.0%（前值-1.1%），家具零售
额同比降幅收窄至 2.6%（前值-3.0%）；另外，10月石油制品零售额同比增长 4.4%（前值-0.8%），主要
与当月成品油价格调升有关。 
二季度以来，社零表现总体走弱，服务消费增速亦有所走低——1-10 月服务零售额累计同比增长
4.9%，增速较前值放缓0.1个百分点，主要与补贴政策退坡、房地产市场延续下行等因素有关。其中，房
地产市场下行是主要拖累，这不仅直接抑制涉房消费，也在持续影响居民消费信心。数据显示，9月消费
者信心指数录得89.0，较8月下滑0.5个点，仍明显低于100的中值水平。同时值得关注的是，今年1-9
月城镇居民人均可支配收入累计同比增长 4.5%，增速较一季度加快 0.3个百分点，但人均可支配收入中
位数同比增速较一季度放缓 0.5个百分点至 3.9%——收入均值与中位数增速背离，表明中低收入群体收
入改善相对滞后，这也是二季度以来消费增速总体放缓的重要原因。 
展望后续，随着“中秋错期”因素消退、上年同期基数走低，预计 11月社零同比增速将有所回升；
同时，前 10个月，旅游咨询租赁服务类、文体休闲服务类零售额明显快于服务零售额增长，对服务消费
带动作用比较明显，预计接下来政策层面还将持续推动文旅、赛事、康养等领域的消费潜力释放。但在
居民消费信心不足背景下，消费偏弱局面短期内仍难以扭转，预计 11月社零同比增速将在 1.6%附近。 
三、投资：1-10月固定资产投资同比增速为-8.1%，降幅较前值扩大 0.9个百分点。9月以来稳投资
政策全面发力，但从资金投放到形成实物工作量需要传导时间，这是 10月基建投资、制造业投资未能遏
制降幅扩大势头的主要原因。近期新房市场延续深度调整，10月房地产投资下行加快，符合预期。不过，
前 10个月知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.2%，高技术产业投资保持较快正增长，显示投资结构优化。

### Page 4

东方金诚宏观研究

4 
1-10月基建投资同比下降 5.1%，降幅比前值扩大 0.9个百分点，不及市场预期。10月 8000亿元新
型政策性金融工具投放完毕；当月安排使用地方政府债务结存限额 5500亿元，其中专项债务结存限额
2500亿元，并向经济大省倾斜，支持有关地区优先保障在建项目资金需求；近期“六张网”建设部署加
快，央行在9月末宣布降低PSL利率，将“六张网”建设纳入支持范围，同时，近期政府债券发行明显提
速，且用于项目建设的资金比例上升。以上稳投资措施均会对基建投资有直接促进作用。不过，上述政
策推进过程中，从资金投放到形成实物工作量需要传导时间，加之受土地出让金大幅下行影响，当前地
方财力普遍偏紧，或是 1-10月基建投资降幅进一步扩大的主要原因。 
往后看，伴随前述稳投资政策效果逐步显现，1-11月基建投资降幅有望收敛至-4.7%左右——数据显
示，10月水泥产量同比下降1.3%，降幅连续两个月收窄，且明显小于前10个月-7.9%的累计降幅。总体
上看，考虑到今年稳投资政策发力时间较去年有所提前，四季度基建投资降幅仍有望显著收窄。这是年
底前“加大逆周期调节力度”的主要体现。 
制造业投资方面，1-10月累计同比增速为-2.9%，降幅较前值扩大 0.8个百分点。背后可能有三个原
因：一是今年 8000亿元新型政策性金融工具支持范围包括先进制造业投资，但从资金投放到形成实物工
作量需要时间。这是 10月制造业投资不及市场预期的主要原因。二是年初以来工业企业利润回暖，主要
得益于全球 AI投资热潮提振电子、有色行业盈利，叠加国际油价上行推高石油石化产业链利润，其他多
数工业行业利润同比下行幅度在加剧。其中，1-9月汽车制造业利润同比下降 22.8%，增速较去年大幅下
行23.4个百分点；这导致1-10月汽车制造业投资同比下降7.9%，而去年全年同比增长11.7%。三是最新
数据显示，2026年三季度工业产能利用率降至 73.1%，环比上升 0.1个百分点，但仍处在近 10年中除
2020年一季度特殊时点外的低位附近，主要受煤炭、水泥、汽车、电气机械和器材制造业等行业产能利
用率下行影响。工业产能利用率偏低会对制造业投资形成较强约束。 
不过，受市场供需两旺及政策倾斜力度较大带动，今年以来高技术制造业生产保持两位数高增，1-10
月高技术产业投资同比增长4.0%，其中，铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业投资增长14.1%，
计算机通信和其他电子设备制造业投资增长 9.9%。另外，1-10月知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.2%，在整
体固定资产投资中的占比已升至 15%左右。这些领域投资高增，不仅有利于当前增加高质量供给，而且能
够夯实长期发展基础，显示当前制造业投资结构在持续改善。往看后，接下来 8000亿新型政策性金融工
具、今年超长期特别国债等支持的制造业项目投资会加快推进，对制造业投资的拉动作用会逐步显现。
我们判断，1-11月制造业投资下行势头会转向收窄。 
房地产投资方面，1-10月累计同比增速为-21.6%，降幅较前值扩大 1.0个百分点。近期房地产投资

### Page 5

东方金诚宏观研究

5 
降幅持续扩大，背后是二季度以来新房市场延续调整。另外，当前金融机构对房企融资仍然偏于谨慎。 
9月 29日，全国范围内针对刚需购房实施定向贴息的政策出台，对楼市有一定提振作用，但主要集
中在二手房市场，10月新房市场仍然偏弱。同时，8月 28日公布系列房地产新政，主要内容包括推动商
品房现售，完善房地产开发贷管理，延长居民按揭贷期限等，核心是将“房住不炒”制度化，加快构建
房地产行业发展新模式。这在引导房地产行业长期稳定发展的同时，会在短期内加大房企资金压力，也
可能是近期房地产投资降幅扩大的一个原因。我们预计，1-11月房地产投资同比降幅有可能扩大至22.0%
左右。总体上看，年底前房地产投资降幅收窄的难度很大。

Document 095: 2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年・冬のボーナス予測 ～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～.pdf
Report source: DLRI

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年 11 月 6 日

1/2 
2026 年・冬のボーナス予測 
～前年比＋3.0％と６年連続の増加を予想～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

○ 民間企業の2026 年冬のボーナス一人当たり支給額を前年比＋3.0％（昨冬同＋2.8％）と予想する
（厚生労働省「毎月勤労統計」ベース）。冬のボーナスとしては６年連続の増加となるだろう。中
東情勢の悪化に伴う資源価格上昇が企業活動の重荷となるなかでも企業収益は高水準を維持してい
る。人手不足を背景とした処遇改善の動きも根強く、今冬もボーナスの増加が続くと予想する。

○ 11 月６日に厚生労働省から公表された26 年夏のボーナス（賞与支給事業所における労働者一人平
均賞与額）は、前年比＋5.3％と５年連続で増加し、昨夏の同＋2.9％から伸びも拡大した。また、
賞与を支給していない事業所も含む「全事業所における労働者一人平均賞与額」で見ても同＋
5.9％と増加している。賞与を支給する事業所の割合も前年の 73.4％から74.0％へ上昇しており、
企業の従業員への還元姿勢が強いことがうかがえる。

○ ボーナスの交渉では、春闘時にその年の年間賞与を決定する夏冬型が採用されていることが多い。
2026 年春闘では、月例給与だけでなく一時金でも増額回答が目立った。企業収益が高水準にある
ことに加え、物価高への配慮や人材確保の観点から、賞与を含めた処遇改善を進める企業が多かっ
たとみられる。また、月例給与の引き上げが賞与の算定基礎額を押し上げ、支給月数の増加と相ま
ってボーナス増につながる面もある。夏のボーナス増に続き、冬についてもこうした交渉結果が反
映される形で増加が予想される。

○ 毎期型の企業、あるいは労働組合が存在しない中小・零細企業においては、より直近の収益状況・
業況がボーナスに反映されやすい。もっとも、イラン情勢悪化によるコスト上昇が企業収益の下押
し要因となるなかでも、価格転嫁の進展などを背景として企業収益は総じて底堅く推移している。
利益の水準も高く、従業員への還元余力はなお保たれているとみてよいだろう。

○ 人手不足感が強いことも、ボーナス増加を後押しする。企業にとって人材の確保・定着は引き続き
重要な経営課題となっており、月例給与だけでなく賞与を含めた処遇改善の必要性は高い。物価高
によって家計負担が増加するなか、従業員への還元を求める声も強い。こうした点も今冬のボーナ
ス増加を支える要因となる。

○ 労務行政研究所が９月30 日に公表した調査によれば、東証プライム上場企業 175 社の 2026 年年末

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
一時金妥結額は、前年比＋4.2％と５年連続で増加した。製造業が同＋4.3％、非製造業が同＋
3.5％と、いずれもプラスとなっている。大企業中心の調査であり、中小企業やパートタイム労働
者も含む毎月勤労統計とは対象が異なるものの、冬のボーナスも堅調に増加するという見方を裏付
ける結果といえる。

○ 冬のボーナス増加は家計の所得環境にとって好材料であり、個人消費を下支えすることが期待され
る。一方で懸念されるのが物価上昇の影響である。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇の価格転嫁など
を背景として、今後は物価上昇率が高まる可能性が高く、政府による電気・ガス代支援の効果を織
り込んでも、2027 年初にはＣＰＩコアの前年比上昇率が＋３％台に達することが予想される。物
価上昇による家計の購買力の圧迫が続くなか、ボーナス増加による個人消費の押し上げ効果につい
ては過度な期待は禁物だろう。

2026 年三季度货币政策执
行报告学习体会

货币政策趋稳   
2026 年三季度货币政策执行报告内容改动之处不多，我们认为，一定程度上意
味着货币政策中长期目标“建设金融强国”成为未来一段时间内的主要任务。 
 2026 年三季度货币政策执行报告中，相比二季度执行报告改动的内容不多： 
 一是对世界经济的判断，从“动能疲弱”变为“韧性不足”，以及出现了“主要经济
体经济表现分化”，对于通胀和货币政策，二季度中提到的“全球通胀水平有所抬升，
主要经济体央行货币政策趋向调整”，也调整成了三季度的“通胀走势和货币政策调
整存在不确定性”。我们认为主要原因在于两方面，一是 2026 年带动全球经济增长的
人工智能科技发展，随着美国中期选举结果落定，开始遭受监管压力，二是推升 2026
年通胀的国际能源价格，也有可能随着美国对外战略的调整，结束供给紧张的格局。
但是从全球范围内来看，一方面通胀有效回落需要时间，另一方面经济增长动力切换
不太容易，因此不排除在特定时间内，全球经济增长韧性面临“通胀处于高位、经济
增速下行、货币政策收紧”的三重考验。 
 二是在国内经济形势判断的部分，二季度的判断是“经济长期向好的内在支撑更加坚
实”，三季度则提出“仍面临供强需弱、结构分化、外部冲击等问题和挑战”。我们
认为这一提法对应的是我国经济增长表现出的两个特点，一是从内部来看，与全球经
济增长类似的 K 型分化，以电子行业为代表的人工智能产业链，对我国进出口、工业
增加值、工业企业利润数据都形成较明显的拉动，二是从外部影响看，2026 年前三个
季度三驾马车中净出口的累计同比贡献率逐步上升。因此无论是从产业链角度，或全
球化角度，我国经济都更容易受全球经济增长的影响。 
 三是关于增量政策和政策协同，二季度货币政策执行报告中提到的是“充分发挥各项
存量政策效能，及时谋划出台务实管用的增量政策”，“加强与财政政策协同配合”，
“发挥好货币政策工具总量和结构双重功能”，三季度则是“发挥存量政策与增量政
策的集成效应”和“发挥好各类结构性货币政策工具作用”，三季度货币政策执行报
告中，增加了“‘六张网’建设”作为金融支持的重点领域之一。我们认为该部分内
容的调整，主要指向的是货币政策的“效能”而非一直以来货币政策宽松指向的“增
量”，可能较大程度上会降低市场对于货币政策持续宽松的预期。 
 四是货币政策改革更加具体，二季度货币政策执行报告中提到“推动贷款定价基准多
元化”，在三季度货币政策执行报告中已经具体为“推动贷款利率定价基准从比较单
一地参考 LPR 定价向更加多元的定价基准转型”，并且“目前已有参考 DR001 定价
的贷款陆续落地”。我们认为现阶段货币政策改革的目标不仅是降低实体经济融资成
本，同时也是加强货币政策传导的改革。 
 五是专栏的部分依旧是五个，我们比较关注的是“专栏 3 设立债市非银大额赎回互换
便利”。“专栏 2 国际利率水平比较”也有提到，我国当前利率、利差，无论是与历
史水平相比还是与海外相比，都处于偏低水平，因此货币政策工具创设和使用的一个
重要考虑，都是“为经济稳定增长、高质量发展和金融市场平稳运行营造适宜的货币
金融环境”。 
 我们认为下一阶段货币政策的重要任务是聚焦完成中长期目标，也就是“金融强国”
建设。 
 三季度我国货币政策大事包括：7 月 24 日，中国人民银行联合科技部等 8 部门印发《关
于加强科技金融领域数据开发利用的通知》；8 月 14 日，中国人民银行、国家外汇管
理局联合发布《关于跨国公司本外币跨境资金集中运营业务有关事宜的通知》；8 月
28 日，中国人民银行会同金融监管总局印发《关于改革完善房地产信贷管理 推动加
快构建房地产发展新模式的意见》；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行联合金融监管总局、中
国证监会、国家发展改革委、工业和信息化部、财政部、商务部、文化和旅游部印发
《关于金融支持服务业扩能提质的指导意见》；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行印发《关于
抵押补充贷款政策加力支持“六张网”建设有关事宜的通知》，下调抵押补充贷款利率
0.25 个百分点至 1.50%，将“六张网”建设纳入抵押补充贷款支持领域；9 月 28 日，中
国人民银行办公厅印发《关于加大科技创新和技术改造再贷款政策支持力度有关事宜
的通知》，增加科技创新和技术改造再贷款额度 2000 亿元，并将该项再贷款支持比例
从 60%提高至 100%；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行决定增加支农支小再贷款额度 5000
亿元，其中民营企业再贷款额度增加 3000 亿元，激励引导地方法人金融机构扩大对涉
农、小微和民营企业的信贷投放；9 月 29 日，财政部会同中国人民银行、金融监管总
局印发《关于实施居民购房贷款贴息政策的通知》。此外，还有同部分国家续签双边
本币互换协议，以及任命海外人民币业务清算行事宜。 
 我们认为下一阶段货币政策可能采取对内“稳”对外“进”的方针：对国内经济和金融市
场以稳定为主，重点在于营造适宜经济高质量发展和平稳运行的货币金融环境，货币
政策方面，增量政策工具可能减少，政策重点在于发挥存量政策效能和加大现有货币
政策应用；对国外以推进人民币国际化、打造“人民币-人民币资产”循环为主，保持人
民币汇率弹性，不断增强人民币和人民币资产对国际资本的吸引力。 
 风险提示：全球通胀二次上行；全球经济增长动能减弱；地缘政治局势发展前景的不
确定性。

当前外部环境更加复杂多变，世界经济增长韧性不足，地缘冲突
和经贸摩擦多发频发，主要经济体经济表现分化，通胀走势和
货币政策调整存在不确定性。我国经济主要指标积极变化增多，
高质量发展取得新成效，但仍面临供强需弱、结构分化、外部
冲击等问题和挑战，既要坚定信心，保持高质量发展战略定力，
也要切实巩固拓展经济稳中向好态势。 
当前外部环境更加复杂多变，世界经济增长动能疲弱，地缘冲
突和经贸摩擦多发频发，供给冲击和输入型通胀压力影响仍在
持续，全球通胀水平有所抬升，主要经济体央行货币政策趋向
调整。我国经济高质量发展取得新成效，后劲持续增强，经济
长期向好的内在支撑更加坚实，制度优势和大国优势不断彰显，
要坚定信心，保持高质量发展战略定力，切实巩固拓展经济稳
中向好态势。 
坚持稳中求进工作总基调，完整准确全面贯彻新发展理念，发挥
存量政策与增量政策的集成效应，加大逆周期调节力度，加力
扩大内需、优化供给，推动经济持续向新向优向好发展，努力完
成全年经济社会发展目标任务。坚定不移走中国特色金融发展
之路，进一步深化金融改革和高水平对外开放，加快建设金融强
国，完善中央银行制度，构建科学稳健的货币政策体系和覆盖全
面的宏观审慎管理体系，畅通货币政策传导机制。 
坚持稳中求进工作总基调，完整准确全面贯彻新发展理念，充分
发挥各项存量政策效能，及时谋划出台务实管用的增量政策，
加大逆周期调节力度，加力扩大内需、优化供给，推动经济持
续向新向优向好发展。坚定不移走中国特色金融发展之路，进

审慎管理体系，畅通货币政策传导机制。

坚持实施好适度宽松的货币政策，为经济稳定增长、高质量发
展和金融市场平稳运行营造适宜的货币金融环境。 继续实施好适度宽松的货币政策。 
增强政策前瞻性灵活性针对性，根据国内外经济金融形势和金融
市场运行情况，综合运用并适时调整货币政策工具，把握好政
策实施的力度、节奏和时机，保持流动性充裕和社会融资条件相
对宽松，引导社会融资规模、货币供应量增长同经济增长、价格
总水平预期目标相匹配。 
增强政策前瞻性灵活性针对性，根据国内外经济金融形势和金融
市场运行情况，把握好政策实施的力度、节奏和时机，加强与财
政政策协同配合，支持经济稳定增长、高质量发展和金融市场
平稳运行。 
综合运用并适时调整货币政策工具，保持流动性充裕和社会融
资条件相对宽松，引导社会融资规模、货币供应量增长同经济增
长、价格总水平预期目标相匹配。 
稳妥有序推动货币政策操作框架改革完善，更好引导货币市场短
端利率围绕政策利率平稳运行。发挥市场利率定价自律机制作
用，加强利率政策执行和监督，推动贷款利率定价基准从比较
单一地参考贷款市场报价利率（LPR）定价向更加多元的定价
基准转型，持续深化明示企业贷款综合融资成本工作，降低融资
中间费用，促进社会综合融资成本低位运行。 
平稳有序推动货币政策操作框架改革完善，更好引导货币市场
短端利率围绕政策利率平稳运行。发挥市场利率定价自律机制
作用，加强利率政策执行和监督，推动贷款定价基准多元化，
引导金融机构提高利率定价能力，持 续深化明示企业贷款综合融
资成本工作，降低融资中间费用，促进社会综合融资成本低位运
行。

发挥好各类结构性货币政策工具作用，不断完善工具设计和管
理，扎实做好金融“五篇大文章”，加强对扩大内需、科技创新、
中小微企业、“六张网”建设等重点领域的金融支持。持续做好
支持民营经济发展壮大的金融服务。 
发挥好货币政策工具总量和结构双重功能，落实好年初出台的
一系列货币金融政策，不断完善工具设计和管理，扎实做好金
融“五篇大文章”，加强对扩大内需、科技创新、中小微企业等重
点领域的金融支持。

坚持以市场供求为基础、参考一篮子货币进行调节、有管理的浮
动汇率制度，保持汇率弹性，发挥汇率调节宏观经济和国际收支
自动稳定器功能，综合施策，增强外汇市场韧性，稳定市场预期，
防范汇率超调风险，保持人民币汇率在合理均衡水平上的基本稳
定。 
坚持以市场供求为基础、参考一篮子货币进行调节、有管理的浮
动汇率制度，保持汇率弹性，发挥汇率调节宏观经济和国际收支
自动稳定器功能，综合施策，增强外汇市场韧性，稳定市场预期，
防范汇率超调风险，保持人民币汇率在合理均衡水平上的基本稳
定。

拓展丰富中央银行宏观审慎和金融稳定功能，完善工具箱，维护
金融市场平稳运行，持之以恒做好风险防范化解工作，坚决守
住不发生系统性金融风险的底线。 
拓展丰富中央银行宏观审慎和金融稳定功能，创新金融工具，维
护金融市场平稳运行，坚决守住不发生系统性金融风险的底线。  
专
栏

财务权益；本报告评论的上市公司或其它第三方都没有或没有承诺向本人提供与本报告有关的任何补偿或其它利益。 
中银国际证券股份有限公司同时声明，将通过公司网站披露本公司授权公众媒体及其他机构刊载或者转发证券研究报

评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

26Q3 受惠終端需求強勁，營收達 81.10 億元，QoQ 8.01%，YoY 
35.27%；受惠稼動率提升與價格策略帶動毛利走高，單季 EPS 達
3.48 元。10 月營收延續強勁動能，達 28.50 億元，MoM 4.79%，
YoY 35.66%，累計前 10 月營收為 248.48 億元，YoY 25.11%。
目前整體市場供不應求，訂單能見度已直達 2027 年中。 
展望後市，隨公司下半年增聘逾 300 名員工並持續加班生產，26
年底產出量將較年初成長 25%至 30%，預期 26Q4 將打破歐美長
假與盤點淡季效應，營運有望優於 26Q3。研究部預估 26Q4 營收
為 85.10 億元，QoQ 4.94%，YoY 30.86%；毛利率估為 35.11%，
營業利益 12.53 億元，QoQ -13.32%，YoY 297.33%；單季 EPS
為 2.95 元。綜合考量下游客戶需求持續暢旺、公司優先承接高毛
利訂單使獲利結構性優化，並已長期佈局機器人與 CPO 商機，研
究部預估 26 與 27 年營收為 305.08 億元與 344.40 億元，YoY 
25.74%與 12.89%；毛利率為 34.54%與 35.11%；營業利益為 43.63
億元與 58.60 億元，YoY 165.52%與 34.30%；稅後獲利為 36.93
億元與 48.07 億元，YoY 142.05%與 30.15%；預估 26 與 27 年
EPS 分別為 10.44 元與 13.59 元。 
評價方面，上銀過去五年本益比主要集中於 20 倍至 40 倍區間。
考量公司整體營收與獲利將呈現雙位數之成長上升趨勢，且自動化
與傳動產業結構持續復甦上行，研究部以 2027 年預估 EPS 13.59
元作為評價基礎，並給予歷史區間中緣偏上之 33 倍 PER，目標價
設定為 450 元，給予買進之投資建議。

⚫ 訂單滿載，能見度達 2027 年中 
估計 2026/2027 年 EPS 為 10.44/13.59 元。 
目前下游需求仍呈現供不應求，研磨級滾珠螺桿交期已拉長至 
6.5 個月以上，線性滑軌依產品規格亦維持 4～6 個月，整體
訂單能見度直達 2027 年中，且 26Q3 合約負債達 1.34 億
元（YoY +126%）。 
各區域表現方面，亞洲市場受惠中國大陸工具機產業升級，帶
動傳動元件需求持續成長，同時台灣與亞洲各國受惠半導體製
造需求熱絡，推升機器人、馬達與旋轉轉台等零組件出貨；美
國受惠製造業回流、半導體擴產與 AI 資料中心建置潮，帶動
強勁設備需求；歐洲雖受車市疲軟影響而整體持平，但軍工與
潛在戰後重建需求已逐步浮現。 
獲利與產能方面，管理層表示現階段不傾向全面漲價，而採取

⚫ 長期佈局機器人與 CPO 商機 
機器人業務佔 26Q3 營收達 12%，其中 EFEM 隨半導體需
求熱絡穩健成長，並已超前部署對應 FOPLP 方片規格之移載
系統。公司充分發揮垂直整合優勢，自製減速機、螺桿、軸承，
並整合關係企業大銀微系統之伺服馬達與驅動器。目前已獲美
系人形機器人廠商小批量線性滑軌訂單，美系 AI 物流機器人
放量時程則取決於客戶端實測進度。CPO 方面，大銀微已開
發出奈米級定位平台，並整合上銀微小型螺桿與線軌，共同搶
攻次世代半導體設備商機。

### Page 1

M
Idea
Muted momentum in near term: Chenbro's 3Q26 earnings fell short of market
expectations by ~30% on lower revenue and margins, owing to customer inventory
digestion, a longer qualification process for its ASIC customer's new product and a
less favorable revenue mix. Looking to 4Q26,  management guided for revenue to
stay roughly flat QoQ (Oct at NT$2,308mn, -7% MoM/+16% YoY) and GM to be
similar to that in 3Q26, as the overhang of new product qualification extends into

4Q26.
2027 still seen as a good year: We believe it continue to grow along with the new
AI server platforms, its customer's share gains and incremental contribution from
new products. We now model for revenue to grow by 35% YoY, GM to be roughly
flat at 29.3% and EPS to rise 39% YoY for 2027.
More diversification into the AI clusters: Chenbro has been leveraging its existing
expertise in mechanical components and customer relationship to diversify from

chassis into other mechanical components in AI applications. It started to see more
meaningful contribution from racks at 5-10% in 2025, with ~10% likely in 2026 and
potentially topping 20% in 2027. For the longer term, it could step into other
mechanical components in the liquid cooling space. Although these efforts could
open up more opportunities for Chenbro to grow, they don't all carry higher-than-
average margins, so potential dilution could be a factor to monitor.

Global footprint continues to expand: To improve its supply chain resilience and
local production capability, Chenbro has several capacity expansion plans globally,
with  Taiwan remaining  the hub for high-end production and R&D. In the pipeline,
Malaysia will likely see new capacity ramping from 4Q26, Vietnam from 2H27 and
US pilot run/MP in 4Q27/2H28. Capex was guided at NT$2.5-3.0bn for 2026.
Downgrade to EW: We believe Chenbro should remain one of the competitive

players in the AI mechanical component space, but near-term hiccups might mute
business momentum. Despite valuation approaching its trough, we see no strong
catalyst in the coming quarters. We thus reduce our rating to EW and lower our PT
by 61% to NT$630 (12x 2027e P/E, implying a PEG of 0.3 vs. the average of 0.5 in
2020-25).
What’s Changed

in the near term, and thus downgrade the stock.

Results Summary
Off revenue of NT$7,395mn (-5% QoQ/+31% YoY), GM contracted by 6.8ppt QoQ to
25.8%, mainly due to a less-favorable product mix and costs associated with new product
qualification. This was short of MSe at 31.9% and consensus at 30.2%, or by 6.1ppt and
4.3ppt, respectively.
Opex was NT$546mn (opex ratio of 7.4%) vs. MSe of NT$686mn (opex ratio 8.2%) and
consensus of NT$617mn (opex ratio 7.5%), leading to operating profit of NT$1,373mn

(OPM 18.6%). This was 31% lower than MSe at NT$1,996mn (OPM 23.9%) and 26% lower
than consensus at NT$1,854mn (OPM 22.6%).
With a non-op gain of NT$2mn (FX gain of NT$18mn), net profit of NT$1,005mn (EPS NT
$8.21) was 33% lower than MSe at NT$1,500mn (EPS NT$12.25) and 29% below
consensus at NT$1,412mn (EPS NT$11.25).

### Page 6

Idea
6
Valuation Methodology
Our price target of NT$630 (previously NT$1,600) is derived from 12x 2027e P/E (vs.
27x 2027e previously): We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it appropriately captures Chenbro's earnings growth potential. We lower our
target multiple to factor in weaker market sentiment toward the stock on softer
fundamental performance and overall to AI names, as well as muted near-term business
momentum. We believe our target P/E multiple of 12x is justified by an earnings CAGR of

32% seen for 2025-28e, implying a PEG of 0.4. This is lower than the PEG of 0.8 in 2014-
24, when the stock traded at an average P/E of 12x for a 15% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value - NT$1,600 (-29%): We apply a 30x P/E multiple (38x 2027e
previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes a better
outlook for AI capex and more project wins from  new customers.
Bear case scenario value - NT$380 (-42%): We apply a 7x P/E multiple (11x 2027e

previously) to our base case estimate for 2027. This scenario mainly assumes lukewarm
datacenter demand amid softening IT capex budgets and slower progress in its new
product offerings.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

MPI Corp. (6223.TWO): Richer MEMS mix to drive GM expansion ahead; 
3Q26 beat; TP up to NT$8,600; Buy
12 November 2026 | 12:27AM HKT
Oct revenues on track: Oct revenues came in at NT$2,232mn, +93% YoY and +9% 
MoM (vs. 3Q26 revenues +13% QoQ), accounting for 33% of our 4Q26E revenue 
forecast. Looking into 4Q26, we expect revenue to grow 13% QoQ on continued 
AI-driven demand for both VPC and MEMS probe cards and initial shipments to the 
US CPU customer, and we raise our GM assumption to 62.4% (vs. 60.4% previously) 
to reflect the richer VPC/MEMS mix seen in 3Q26. For 2027, we expect MEMS 
capacity additions, share gains at the US CPU customer and rising pin count per card 
to drive growth, with GM expanding further as MEMS mix rises. 3Q26 beat: Revenue 
was pre-announced. GM of 61.7% came in above GSe/Bloomberg consensus of 
59.8%/59.5%, which we attribute to higher VPC/MEMS shipments for AI-related 
applications. Together with a lower opex ratio of 23.1% (vs. GSe/cons. of 
23.6%/24.1%) on larger revenue scale, operating income grew 26% QoQ, 8.5% / 
11.5% ahead of GSe/cons. EPS of NT$19.18 was 8.1% / 11.0% above GSe/cons. With 
higher GM, we raise our 2026-28E EPS by 5%/3%/1%, and raise our 12m TP to 
NT$8,600 (implied 53x 2027E P/E vs. 90% NI YoY of 2027-28E on avg.). Maintain Buy.

MEMS capacity ramp and new customers to drive growth: Our 4Q26E forecast 
implies average monthly revenue of NT$2,226mn in November-December (flat vs. 
October). On the margin front, we expect product mix to remain favorable for GM, as 
VPC/MEMS capacity additions and new customer ramps continue. Longer term, we 
remain positive on MPI’s growth outlook, driven by 1) MEMS and VPC capacity 
expansion, where we keep our capacity assumption of 2.5mn/8.0mn pins per month 
for VPC/MEMS by end-2027E, which we see as well supported as multiple AI ASIC

and networking programs move into volume; 2) share gains at the US CPU customer, 
where we see potential for it to become MPI’s top revenue contributor in 2027, 
supported by strong CPU demand from agentic AI; 3) content growth, as pin count per 
card keeps climbing, further lift by micro-bump probing at some customers; 4) rising 
PCB insourcing, which should improve both delivery times and margins; and 5) CPO, 
which should open a new growth avenue for its equipment business across Insertion 2 
and 3.

Earnings revisions: We incorporate the 3Q26 results into our model and revise our 
2026/2027/2028E EPS by +4.8%/+3.2%/+1.2%, as we factor in 1) higher GM, reflecting 
the 3Q26 results and a richer VPC/MEMS mix, and 2) higher revenue 
(+0.8%/+1.2%/+1.5% vs. our prior forecasts). Overall, we now model revenue growth of 
63%/106%/47% YoY over 2026-28E (vs. 61%/105%/46% previously), with GM 
expanding to 60.7%/63.1%/63.9% (vs. 59.6%/62.2%/63.7% previously).

Valuation: As a result of our earnings revisions, we raise our 12-month TP to NT$8,600 
(from NT$8,500). Our 12-month TP is still based on a target P/E of 40x (1.7x of 3-year 
average forward P/E) applied to our 2028E EPS, and discounted back to 2027E at 12.4% 
CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x beta (sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 
4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 6.25% (in line with GS house view). 
Maintain Buy.

Price Target Risks and Methodology – MPI (6223.TWO) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on MPI. Our 12-month TP of NT$8,600 is based on a target 
P/E of 40x (1.7x of 3-year average forward P/E) applied to our 2028E EPS, and 
discounted back to 2027E at 12.4% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x 
beta (sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 
6.25% (in line with GS house view). 
Key risks to our views: 1) softer AI/HPC demand, 2) slower penetration into new TAM, 
and 3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

Idea
3Q26 GM slightly lower: Off revenue of NT$120,744mn (+26% QoQ/+66% YoY),
GM contracted by 2.1ppt QoQ to 17.6% (vs. MSe and consensus both at 18.5%) on
higher component costs. With opex of NT$5,162mn (opex ratio 4.3% vs. MSe 4.3%/
consensus  4.0%), OP was NT$16,122mn (OPM 13.4%), 6%/5% lower than MSe of NT
$17,183mn (OPM 14.2%)/consensus  of NT$17,047mn (OPM 14.4%), respectively. With
a narrower non-op gain of NT$94mn, net profit came in at NT$13,074mn (EPS NT

$23.4), 2%/5% short of MSe/consensus of NT$13,328mn (EPS NT$23.9)/NT
$13,748mn (EPS NT$24.5).
Outlook still on optimistic side: We expect Accton to grow revenue sequentially
into 4Q26. Network switch customers will likely continue to pull in more for their AI
clusters amid 800G migration. Meanwhile, its AI accelerator customer should ramp
more of its new generation product and increase adoption of liquid cooling thermal
solutions. To mitigate pressure from elevated component costs, it has negotiated

with customers for them to consign some components, e.g., DRAM, from late 3Q26.
Although this might cap revenue growth upside for Accton, it should help on the
GM side. We model revenue of NT$130,083mn (+8% QoQ/+81% YoY), gross margin
of 20.0% (+2.4ppt QoQ), operating margin of 15.5% (+2.1ppt QoQ)  and EPS of NT
$28.2 (+21% QoQ/+88% YoY). Looking to 2027, we see revenue growth of NT
$526,019mn (+26% YoY) and GM of 20.0%, for EPS NT$112.9 EPS (+31% YoY).

Stay OW: We believe Accton should continue to benefit from networking and
compute demand growth amid AI deployment, reflecting its system integration
capability, customer relationships, and execution. For the longer term,  it will likely
tap into opportunities in rack level solutions, optical-based switches, etc. We lower
our PT to NT$2,250 (20x 2027 P/E) from NT$3,500 (30x 2027 P/E), mostly owing
to more conservative sentiment toward AI names.

3Q26 GM Fell Short Slightly;
Better Margin Trend Ahead
Although 3Q26 earnings came up short on lower GM, we expect
Accton to deliver high single digit % revenue growth with GM
back to ~20% in 4Q26, reflecting some component consignment
arrangements.  Margin expansion in 4Q26 should be a positive
catalyst for the share price in the coming months.

6
Valuation Methodology and Risks
OW with PT of NT$2,250 (previously NT$3,500)
Price target (base case scenario value) –  NT$2,250 (-36%): Our new price target is based
on 20x our 2027 EPS estimate (30x previously). We lower our target multiple to factor in
recent market sentiment toward AI names. We see our target multiple supported by the
45% operating profit CAGR we project for 2025-28. This implies a PEG of 0.4 vs. its
average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for an operating

profit CAGR of 32%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,350 (-20%): We apply 30x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (36x previously). This scenario reflects stronger demand for network switches in
both data center and enterprise applications as well as more orders for AI accelerator
modules.
Bear case scenario value – NT$1,800 (-39%): We apply 10x to our base case EPS estimate
for 2027 (16x previously). This scenario reflects lukewarm data center and enterprise

capex and continued dominance by merchant GPUs in AI servers.
Downside risks to our positive thesis
• Weaker data center capex
• Slower network switch spec migration
• InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
• Softer custom AI accelerator growth
• Softer ODM direct business model adoptions

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
4Q NB build forecast cut 5% despite October’s beat: October ODM NB shipments
came in 4% above MSe, but we lower our 4Q26 build forecast by 5% to 21.9M units
(-13% q/q, -32% y/y) to reflect the latest ODM guidance. The revised forecast implies

limited quarter-end pull-in strength and a deeper y/y contraction than the 26%
decline in 3Q26. Despite an already depressed 3Q base, the projected 13%
sequential decline is well below the 15-year average of flat q/q shipments in 4Q.
PC outlook seen continuing to weaken into 2027: NB build trends have weakened
over recent months, while increasingly cautious ODM commentary provides further
evidence of pressure on PC demand. Asustek expects global PC unit shipments to

decline 10–15% y/y in 2027 (link), while Quanta expects global NB shipments to
drop by high single digits to 10% y/y (link). We believe 2027 outlooks from the
supply chain have worsened in recent months, and both outlooks are directionally
consistent with our forecast for an 11.5% y/y decline in global PC units in 2027.
Looking to 1Q27, our preliminary view is a further sequential decline in NB builds,
although we have yet to establish a formal forecast.

Key Takeaways
Top five ODMs' October 2026 NB shipments were 7.1M units (-27% m/m, -30% y/
y), 4% above our estimate.
We see November 2026 NB builds at 7.3M units (+4% m/m, -30% y/y).
We lower our 4Q26 NB build estimate by 5% to 21.9M units (-13% q/q, -32% y/y).
Our top five ODMs' CY26 NB build estimate is ~106.6M units (-17% y/y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

2026.11.13 
洪有辰 C00270@ibfs.com.tw 
3090.TT 日電貿 
 庫存等待進一步消化，日正當中再續衝

[財務資訊更新，法說會更新]   
   公司簡介 
日電貿成立於 1993 年，為台灣規模最大的被動元件代理商，其代理的企業品牌涵蓋
美國、日本與韓國等多家知名被動元件與電子零組件公司，產品線以各種電容、電感、
半導體元件與其他光電元件等為主，近期則開始聚焦於高規格電容類產品。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 172 
【前次投資建議】強力買進，256 
【大盤指數/股價】43092 / 132.5

➢ 因業外減少，3Q26 EPS 降至 2.46 元，低於投研部預期 
3Q26 營收 58.61 億元，QoQ+13.7%、YoY+44.7%；受惠 AI 伺服器及
資料中心需求強勁，帶動 MLCC 等高價值產品出貨成長，毛利率提升至
21.12%，QoQ+3ppts，YoY+5.2ppts；EPS 2.49 元，獲利呈現季衰退
並低於投研部預期，主因為 2Q26 存在一筆業外股利收入 4.5 億元。 
➢ 4Q26 客戶放緩拉貨節奏，且大客戶佔比上升，營收與獲利續降 
受惠 AI 高階 MLCC 供需緊俏，以及日韓原廠產能轉移，部分消費性產
品交期已延長至 24～36 週，預期 4Q26 AI 與一般型產品報價同步調漲，
MLCC 營收占比可望由 52%升至 54%。預估 4Q26 營收 58.17 億元，
QoQ-0.8% 、 YoY+52.5% ；毛利率 20.64% ， QoQ-0.5ppts ，
YoY+2.7ppts；EPS 2.40 元。惟 2026 年底客戶控管庫存，加上大型客
戶議價能力較強，獲利率略受稀釋。 
➢ 供需失衡延續至 2027 年，漲價潮與 AI 占比破六成將使獲利再升 
日電貿因輝達 Vera Rubin 平台出貨遞延，客戶拉貨速度低於預期，導致
3Q26 存貨大幅攀升至 46.03 億元，主要反映高階元件 ASP 上升及長交
期產品備貨需求，惟隨 2027 年初 Rubin 放量，庫存可望逐步去化。展
望 2027 年，AI 與傳統伺服器需求同步成長，高階 MLCC 因製程難度
高、良率偏低，供給持續緊俏，預估 MLCC 營收占比升至 57%，年增
49.7%；固態電容及電解電容營收亦分別成長 44.7%及 34.9%。隨著 AI
營收占比提升至約 60%，加上原廠漲價及產品組合優化，毛利率有望重
拾上漲動能。 
➢ 預估 2027 年 EPS 14.37 元，給予買進評等，目標價 172 元 
投研部預估日電貿於 2026 年/2027 年營收 213.24 億元/304.43 億元，
YoY+35.58%/+42.76% ； 毛 利 率 19.99%/22.15% ， YoY+3.6ppts 
/2.2ppts；稅後 EPS 9.80 元/14.37 元，目前 PER 約在 13.5X/9.2X，參
考過去五年 PER 區間大部分在 10X~18X，目前股價以 2027 年衡量低
於區間下緣，考量公司獨家代理的多項高階產品因供需持續失衡而有望
漲價，使 AI 佔比持續上升，故給予買進評等，但下修目標價至 172 元
(2027F PE 12X)。

### Page 2

- 2 -

個股報告

3Q26 因業外減少，EPS 降至 2.49 元，低於投研部預期 
日電貿 3Q26 營收 58.61 億元，QoQ+13.7%、YoY+44.7%，主因為 AI 伺服器、資
料中心及電源相關需求同步增加，帶動陶瓷電容(MLCC)等高附加價值產品出貨成長，
以營收結構來看，公司三大類別分別為 MLCC 佔 52%、固態電容(SCP)佔 26%、電解
電容(ECP)佔 9%。此外，SCP 佔比下滑至 26%，因客戶擔憂缺貨而於 2Q26 提前備
貨，加上 3Q26 MLCC 成長速度更快所致；毛利率提升至 21.12%，QoQ+3ppts，
YoY+5.2ppts，呈現出 AI 伺服器與資料中心需求強勁，帶動 MLCC 與各類電容產品
出貨上升的現象；然而稅前淨利 8.98 億元，QoQ-13.3%，主因為 2Q26 存在一筆業
外股利收入 4.5 億元；EPS 2.49 元，QoQ-22.4%，YoY+26.4%。3Q26 營收低於投
研部預期，因公司客戶於 2Q26 提前備貨，故 3Q26 季增幅回歸較穩定狀態；獲利亦
因上述的業外減少而低於預期。累計前三季 EPS 7.4 元，YoY+76.2%。 
圖 1：日電貿產品組合佔比預估

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
2026 年底客戶放緩拉貨節奏，且大客戶佔比上升，4Q26 營收與獲利續降 
日電貿目前因 AI 用的高階 MLCC 供需緊俏，一般型產品也受到日韓原廠產能轉移影
響，部分消費性產品交期已拉長至 24~36 週，預期 4Q26 公司旗下 AI 與一般型料號
報價將調漲，帶動相關營收隨之上升，因此 MLCC 比重有望由 52%進一步升至 54%。
投研部預估 4Q26 營收 58.17 億元，QoQ-0.8%，YoY+52.5%，季衰退的原因推測為
公司客戶於 2026 年底進行盤點並控管庫存，故傾向放緩拉貨腳步；毛利率 20.64%，
QoQ-0.5ppts，YoY+2.7ppts，儘管 AI 占比將超過 50%，但大型客戶占比也持續增
加，公司對這些客戶的產品議價與通路利潤率相對較低，故毛利率有所稀釋；營業利
益率 13.97%，QoQ-0.85ppts，YoY+3.2ppts；稅後 EPS 2.40 元，QoQ-3.5%，
YoY+89.3%。 
MLCC 供需失衡延續至 2027 年，漲價潮與 AI 占比破六成將使獲利再升 
目前輝達(NVDA.US) Vera Rubin 平台出貨遞延，使日電貿的客戶拉貨速度低於預期，
因此在 3Q26 季底累積的庫存金額高達 46.03 億元，且高階元件 ASP 上升，再加上
這些產品的交期較長而需建立更安全的庫存水位，因此相較過往才會異常飆高。公司
認為未來一至兩季需求仍佳，待 Rubin 於 2027 年初開始大量拉貨後，庫存水位可望
下降並轉化為營收，同時存貨週轉天數也從目前的 86 天逐季下降。 
過去兩年，AI 伺服器市場快速成長，傳統伺服器需求則相對溫和；然而，隨著企業 IT

### Page 3

- 3 -

個股報告 
支出回溫與 CSP 大廠持續擴大資本支出，預期自 2026 年~2027 年，傳統伺服器與
AI 伺服器將同步成長，帶動整體伺服器產業規模持續擴大。就市場規模而言，全球伺
服器於 2027 年預估約達 1.1 兆美元，並於 2028 年再提升至 1.6 兆美元，年增幅約
15%。其中，輝達 NVL72 機櫃將成為重要成長動能，2028 年相關市場產值可達約 1
兆美元，占整體市場約 63%；另一方面，ASIC 伺服器近年出貨量快速增加，預估至
2028 年市場滲透率將提升至 65%，顯示雲端業者正逐步擴大自研晶片的部署。從晶
片出貨量來看，2025 年 ASIC 約為 430 萬顆，輝達與 AMD 相關 GPU 則約 550 萬
顆；至 2028 年，ASIC 出貨量預估將增至 2,220 萬顆，較 2025 年成長約 5.2 倍，
GPU 相關出貨量則可達 1,610 萬顆，成長約 2.9 倍。整體而言，未來伺服器市場將由
傳統伺服器復甦、AI 訓練與推論需求擴張，以及 ASIC 自研晶片加速滲透等多重因素
共同推動，顯示 GPU 與 ASIC 兩大運算架構將呈現同步成長，而非單純的替代關係。 
圖 2：日電貿存貨金額 單位：億元

資料來源：國票投顧整理 
AI 伺服器所需的高階 MLCC，因產品規格涉及高容值、高耐壓與高可靠度要求，製程
難度明顯高於一般規格產品，導致產品良率相對偏低。相較一般 MLCC 良率可達
99.9%，部分 AI 高階產品良率依產品別與不同客戶的設計差異約落在 40%~80%不
等，使生產相同數量的合格品，需投入更多製造資源，因此形成較大的產能消耗。儘
管如此，受惠 AI 伺服器與高效能運算需求持續擴張，高階 MLCC 訂單仍相當充足，
供給短期內不易快速增加。日電貿目前客戶需求都維持強勁，尤其 MLCC 產品拉貨動
能明確，因此維持緊俏的狀況將延續至 2027 年，使其成為被動元件產業中具備較高
成長潛力的重要產品。投研部預估 MLCC 佔比將自 2026 年底的 54%上升至 2027 年
的 57%，貢獻營收 YoY+49.7%；固態電容與電解電容雖然分別由 25%下滑至 24%與
9%下滑至 8%，但以貢獻營收衡量，YoY 仍分別增長 44.7%與 34.91%。儘管通路商
的毛利率較其他被動元件製造廠的毛利低，但在 2027 年預期 AI 占比將可望達 60%，
因此仍有望藉由調整旗下客戶去優化產品組合，且代理的原廠漲價也有機會帶動通路
價差增加，故 2027 年毛利率有望再次上揚。 
預估 2027 年 EPS 14.37 元，給予買進評等，目標價 172 元 
投 研 部 預 估 日 電 貿 2026 年 /2027 年 營 收 213.24 億元/304.43 億 元 ，
YoY+35.58%/+42.76%；毛利率為 19.99%/22.15%，YoY+3.6ppts/2.2ppts；稅後
EPS 9.80 元/14.37 元，目前 PER 約在 13.7X/9.2X，參考過去五年 PER 區間大部分在
10X~18X，目前股價以 2027 年衡量低於區間下緣，考量公司獨家代理的多項高階產
品因供需持續失衡而有望漲價，使 AI 佔比持續上升，故給予買進評等，但下修目標價
至 172 元(2027F PE 12X)。

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G는 +11.5%, ASP는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

그림 5. SK하이닉스 투자자별 순매수 추이(2010년부터 누계)  그림 6. SK하이닉스 마이크론 밸류에이션 비교

자료: 에프앤가이드 QuantiWise, IBK투자증권  자료: Bloomberg, IBK투자증권

시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 
SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수

minho.hur@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 85,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
56,700 
유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

자료: 유안타증권

Company Report  2026.11.12

상선, 방산, 해양, 엔진 모든 사업 영위하는 전천후 기업 
경쟁사 대비 1) 가장 넓은 사업 영역을 보유, 2) 시가총액 또한 높음. 이러한 특성은 동사의 
사업 포트폴리오 균형 관점에서 장점이나, 반대로 투자 매력의 집중을 일정 부분 희석. 경쟁
사 대비 주가 상승 트리거가 가장 폭넓게 존재하나, 상승 탄력은 그만큼 제한. 
상선: FY27F 매출인식은 FY23-25 수주 물량, 가스선 매출비중 하락 
FY27F 상선부문 매출은 FY23-25 수주 물량이 인식되며 (일부 FY26F 수주), FY25 수주
한 물량이 연간 40% 수준의 가장 높은 매출기여도를 보일 것. 2H27F부터 올해 수주한 일
부 선박의 매출인식 또한 발생하며, 연중 가스선 매출 비중이 하락하고 컨테이너선 매출 비
중이 상승. 
방산: 좋게 보자면 불안하고, 나쁘게 보자면 아쉬운 
MASGA를 국내 야드에서 진행한다는 대전제로 경쟁사와 같은 적정가치를 부여해 왔으나, 
미국 현지 건조와 같은 여러 가능성을 고려하면 다음과 같은 불확실성 존재: 1) 중간지주사
와의 미국내 JV 설립 관련 투자 지분 미정, 2) 신규 조선소 건립시 미국내 수주와 매출까지 
걸리는 시간. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 다 깎아서 봐도 충분한 상승여력 
상선부문 26.5조, 방산 8조, 선박엔진 9조, 발전엔진 (FY30F 기준) 13.5조 수준의 SOTP 
밸류에이션 기반하여 목표주가 643,000원 제시. 발전엔진 증설로 인한 미래가치를 반영하
나, 상선부문 Target P/O multiple 하향 (0.7x, 업종 공통)과 MASGA 적정가치 하향으로 
목표주가 자체는 하락. 보수적 멀티플 제시함에도 주가 상승 여력은 충분히 확보된 상황. 업
종 전체적 긍정요인 외의 동사 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 미국 현지 조선소 투자주체 발탁, 2) 
DC향 발전엔진 추가 수주, 3) MASGA 국내 건조 물량 수주.

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports.1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.
Exhibit 1: FDI Inflows to the U.S.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026,2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.
A) Higher 10-year Yield Consistent with Strong Nominal Growth Environment. B) Domestic Private Investors Now Dominate U.S. Treasury Market.

Investment Implications
In our view, higher nominal and real
yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.

Index Definitions

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

### Page 1
